# AERIS — Complete Command & Ability Reference

> **Standalone Hinglish AI assistant.** Speak or type in Hindi, English, or a mix — AERIS understands natural phrasing, not just the exact words below. **588 abilities** across 19 categories, all running **on-device** (no cloud, no API keys).

**How to read this:** every ability lists real example phrases. AERIS matches *meaning*, so slight variations ("screenshot lo" ≈ "take a screenshot" ≈ "screen capture karo") all work. Voice and typed input are equivalent.

_This file is generated by `gen_commands_readme.py` — re-run it after adding skills._
